Every other section is written.\n- `contract_pin` is omitted: the backend is the source of the contract.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for.** One read operation, `API-SEARCH-580505` (GET `/notes/search`).\n- It finds notes by keyword across title and body, by tag, or by both combined (US-003-01, US-003-02, US-003-04).\n- It excludes archived notes unless `include_archived=true` (US-003-06, BR-009).\n- It paginates results with a total count (US-003-05, BR-011).\n- It returns pinned notes before unpinned ones (US-003-01, BR-019).\n- It returns 503 with a retry-after header when the search index is degraded (US-003-03).\n- It meets p95 < 300 ms at 50 requests per second (NFR-006, NFR-007, US-003-03).\n\n**Derivation.** The module boundary is `search` in `backend.module-map`. The dependency `search \u2192 notes` is read-only.\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n- Creating, editing, archiving or unarchiving notes, or the `pinned` flag. These belong to `notes` (`backend.module-map`).\n- Adding or removing tags. They belong to `notes`.\n- Tag removal and distinct-tag listing (`GAP-BE-003`, `Q-BE-006`).\n- Listing notes without a filter, such as an active-notes list or an archived-only list. No such operation is defined (`GAP-BE-002`, `Q-BE-005`).\n- Authentication, sessions and the access rule. They belong to `auth` and are blocked (`Q-BE-002`, `Q-BE-003`).\n- The published OpenAPI document and its examples (`contract-docs`).\n- Refusing out-of-scope calls (`scope-guard`).\n- The error envelope and the error-code registry (`backend.error-model`). Retry-after values are open there too (`Q-BE-004`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**Ownership.**\n- `search` owns no table and persists nothing. No input requires a search history, search log or saved query.\n- Notes and note-tag rows are owned by `notes`. Their columns, types, nullability and constraints are defined once in `backend.notes.data-model` and are not restated here.\n- `search` owns only the index structures. They are migration 4 in `backend.migration-plan` (\"Add the search index structures\").\n\n**Fields read by a search** (names from `backend.domain-model`):\n\n| Entity | Fields read | Used for |\n|---|---|---|\n| NOTE | title, body | keyword match (US-003-01) |\n| NOTE | status | archived exclusion (BR-009) |\n| NOTE | pinned | pinned-first ordering (BR-019) |\n| NOTE_TAG | tag_name, note_id | tag filter (US-003-02) |\n\n**Index structures.** The labels are local to this document, not identifiers.\n\n| Structure | Over | Query it serves | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Text index | NOTE title and body | `q` match, under the p95 target at about 100k notes | US-003-01, US-003-03, NFR-006 |\n| Tag index | NOTE_TAG (tag_name, note_id) | `tags` filter. Note-tag rows are unique per note and tag (migration 2, `notes`). | US-003-02, `backend.migration-plan` |\n| Ordering and archive filter | NOTE (pinned, status) | pinned-first order and the archived exclusion | BR-009, BR-019 |\n\n**Open points.**\n- The index technology, type and update mechanism are unstated. `ASM-BE-068` assumes the index lives in the PostgreSQL 16 database (`platform.md` names no other store). `GAP-BE-075` records the rest.\n- Whether a dedicated index on (pinned, status) is needed cannot be decided without data volumes (`GAP-BE-075`).\n- \"Relevance\" has no stated scoring (`GAP-BE-073`).\n- Tag rules are unstated (`GAP-BE-003`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\n**Entity state.**\n- `search` owns no entity with a status column, so it owns no entity state machine.\n- It reads the note `status` (active or archived), which is owned by `notes`. Its transitions are in `backend.notes.state-machine` and are not restated here.\n- Search never changes a status (`INV-BE-051`).\n\n**Index availability.** The only state this module can distinguish is whether the index can answer. US-003-03 distinguishes \"degraded or unavailable\" from normal operation.\n\n| State | Meaning | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| AVAILABLE | the index answers queries | derived from US-003-03 |\n| DEGRADED | the index is degraded or unavailable | US-003-03 |\n\n| Transition | Trigger | Guard | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| AVAILABLE \u2192 DEGRADED | not specified | not specified | `GAP-BE-075` |\n| DEGRADED \u2192 AVAILABLE | not specified | not specified | `GAP-BE-075` |\n\n**Behaviour in each state.**\n- AVAILABLE: validate the parameters, then answer. Invalid parameters give 400.\n- DEGRADED: a valid request gets 503 with a retry-after header (BE-SEARCH-FR-017).\n- An invalid request in DEGRADED gets 400, because validation runs first (`ASM-BE-072`).\n\n**Terminal states.** None. Both states are recoverable, and the recovery mechanism is unspecified.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nAll entries depend on `\u2192 API-SEARCH-580505`. Verification names are proposed (planned). Error codes follow `backend.error-model`, whose names are themselves proposed (`ASM-BE-006`).\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-001: keyword match**\n- Given notes whose title or body matches a keyword,\n- When GET `/notes/search?q=keyword` is called,\n- Then 200 returns the matching notes, pinned notes first, then ordered by relevance.\n- Traces to. US-003-01, BR-019.\n- Verification. `search_keyword_matches_title_or_body` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-002: no keyword match**\n- Given no note matches the keyword,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then 200 returns an empty array, not an error.\n- Traces to. US-003-01.\n- Verification. `search_no_match_returns_empty_200` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-003: no usable filter**\n- Given `q` is missing, empty or whitespace-only, and no `tags` value is supplied,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then 400 returns a validation error.\n- A request with `tags` and no `q` is valid (`CON-BE-002`, `ASM-BE-004`, `ASM-BE-063`).\n- Traces to. US-003-01, US-003-02, BR-010.\n- Verification. `search_without_q_or_tags_400`; `search_tags_only_is_valid` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-004: tag filter**\n- Given notes tagged `project-x` exist,\n- When GET `/notes/search?tags=project-x` is called,\n- Then only notes carrying that tag are returned.\n- Traces to. US-003-02.\n- Verification. `search_by_tag_returns_only_tagged` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-005: tag with no matching notes**\n- Given a tag with no matching notes,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then 200 returns an empty result set.\n- Traces to. US-003-02.\n- Verification. `search_unused_tag_returns_empty_200` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-006: invalid tag characters**\n- Given the `tags` parameter contains invalid characters,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then 400 returns an error detail.\n- The valid character set is unstated (`GAP-BE-003`).\n- Traces to. US-003-02.\n- Verification. `search_tags_with_invalid_chars_400` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-007: combined filters**\n- Given `q=budget` and `tags=finance` are both supplied,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then only notes matching both criteria are returned.\n- Several tags in `tags` follow `ASM-BE-061` and `ASM-BE-062`.\n- Traces to. US-003-04, BR-010.\n- Verification. `search_q_and_tags_requires_both` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-008: combined filters exclude everything**\n- Given the keyword matches but the tag filter excludes all matches,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then an empty result set is returned.\n- Traces to. US-003-04.\n- Verification. `search_combined_no_overlap_returns_empty` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-009: malformed filter combination**\n- Given a malformed filter combination,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then 400 returns error details.\n- \"Malformed\" is not defined by any input beyond FR-003, FR-006, FR-012 and FR-016 (`GAP-BE-077`).\n- Traces to. US-003-04.\n- Verification. `search_malformed_combination_400` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-010: pagination**\n- Given more results exist than the page size,\n- When `page` and `limit` are supplied,\n- Then the correct subset is returned along with a total count.\n- The count is the total across all pages (`ASM-BE-067`).\n- Traces to. US-003-05, BR-011.\n- Verification. `search_pagination_returns_subset_and_total` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-011: page beyond the results**\n- Given a page number beyond the available results,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then 200 returns an empty array.\n- Traces to. US-003-05.\n- Verification. `search_page_beyond_results_returns_empty_200` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-012: invalid paging values**\n- Given `page` or `limit` is negative or non-numeric,\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then 400 returns a validation error.\n- By assumption the same applies to zero, and to a `limit` above the maximum (`ASM-BE-065`).\n- Traces to. US-003-05.\n- Verification. `search_invalid_page_or_limit_400` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-013: bounded result sets**\n- Given a request with or without `limit`,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then the page never exceeds the maximum page size, and a default page size applies when `limit` is absent.\n- Both values follow `ASM-BE-065`.\n- Traces to. BR-011, NFR-007.\n- Verification. `search_default_and_max_page_size_enforced` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-014: archived excluded by default**\n- Given archived and active notes both match,\n- When a default search runs,\n- Then only active notes are returned.\n- Traces to. US-003-06, BR-009.\n- Verification. `search_default_excludes_archived` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-015: archived included on request**\n- Given `include_archived=true`,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then archived notes are included in the results.\n- Traces to. US-003-06, BR-009.\n- Verification. `search_include_archived_true_includes_archived` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-016: invalid `include_archived`**\n- Given an `include_archived` value other than the accepted values (`ASM-BE-066`),\n- When the search request is made,\n- Then 400 returns an error message.\n- Traces to. US-003-06.\n- Verification. `search_invalid_include_archived_400` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-017: degraded index**\n- Given the search index is degraded or unavailable,\n- When a valid search is requested,\n- Then 503 is returned with a retry-after header instead of hanging.\n- The header value is unstated (`Q-BE-004`, `GAP-BE-004`).\n- Traces to. US-003-03.\n- Verification. `search_index_degraded_returns_503_with_retry_after` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-018: latency**\n- Given 50 requests per second, and a dataset of about 100k notes,\n- When searches run,\n- Then p95 latency is under 300 ms, including page sizes up to 100.\n- Traces to. US-003-03, NFR-006, NFR-007.\n- Verification. `search_p95_under_300ms_at_50rps_100k_notes` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-019: ordering**\n- Given any result set, including one with archived notes included,\n- When the results are ordered,\n- Then all pinned notes precede unpinned notes across the whole result set.\n- Within each group, ordering is relevance first (`GAP-BE-073`), then note id (`ASM-BE-064`), so pages are stable.\n- Traces to. US-003-01, BR-019.\n- Verification. `search_pinned_first_across_pages`; `search_order_is_stable_between_pages` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-020: route precedence**\n- Given a parameterised note-get route exists (`backend.notes`),\n- When GET `/notes/search` is called,\n- Then it is handled by search, not as a note id (`ASM-BE-070`).\n- Traces to. US-003-01.\n- Verification. `search_route_not_shadowed_by_note_get` (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**BE-SEARCH-FR-021: input handled as data**\n- Given `q`, `tags` or other parameters containing injection payloads,\n- When the search runs,\n- Then they are treated as data, never as query syntax.\n- Traces to. NFR-012.\n- Verification. `search_injection_payloads_are_inert`; the release security scan (planned).\n- API. \u2192 API-SEARCH-580505\n\n**Story coverage in this module**\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-003-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003, FR-019, FR-020 |\n| US-003-02 | FR-003, FR-004, FR-005, FR-006 |\n| US-003-03 | FR-017, FR-018 |\n| US-003-04 | FR-007, FR-008, FR-009 |\n| US-003-05 | FR-010, FR-011, FR-012, FR-013 |\n| US-003-06 | FR-014, FR-015, FR-016 |\n\nAll six owned stories are covered. Several rest on assumptions listed in `backend.search.gaps`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 503 | Search notes by keyword, by tag, or both combined. Archived notes are excluded unless include_archived is set. Results are paginated with pinned notes first. Returns 503 when the search index is degraded. |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nSearch notes by keyword, by tag, or both combined. Archived notes are excluded unless include_archived is set. Results are paginated with pinned notes first. Returns 503 when the search index is degraded.\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string): Keyword to match against notes. Optional when at least one tag is given (ASM-BE-004, CON-BE-002). A request with neither q nor tags is rejected with 400 (US-003-01).\n- `tags` (array): One or more tags to filter by. When given together with q, both filters apply (US-003-02, BR-010). Tag rules are unstated (GAP-BE-003).\n- `include_archived` (boolean): When true, archived notes are included. Default is false, so archived notes are excluded (BR-009, BR-019). A non-boolean value returns 400.\n- `page` (integer): 1-based page number for pagination (US-003-05, BR-011). The name and base are not stated by an input.\n- `page_size` (integer): Number of results per page, up to 100 (NFR-007). Larger or invalid values return 400. The name and default are not stated by an input.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Matching notes for the requested page. Pinned notes come first, then the remaining notes (BR-019). Each item shows the note id, title, body, pinned flag, status, tags and timestamps.\n- `page` (integer, required): The page number returned.\n- `page_size` (integer, required): The page size applied.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of notes matching the filters across all pages.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-051.** After any search, nothing is changed. No note, tag, status, `pinned` value or login record differs from before. Derived: US-003-01 to US-003-06 describe retrieval only.\n- **INV-BE-052.** Without `include_archived=true`, no returned note has archived status (BR-009).\n- **INV-BE-053.** In any result set, every pinned note precedes every unpinned note (BR-019). This holds across pages.\n- **INV-BE-054.** Every returned note satisfies every supplied filter. A note must match `q` if given, and the tag filter if given (US-003-04, BR-010).\n- **INV-BE-055.** No response contains more notes than the maximum page size. Unbounded result sets are never returned (BR-011, `ASM-BE-065`).\n- **INV-BE-056.** A request with an invalid parameter never gets 200, and a request against a degraded index never gets 200. They get 400 and 503 respectively (US-003-01 to US-003-06; US-003-03).\n- **INV-BE-057.** Paging is deterministic. For an unchanged data set, the same request returns the same page (`ASM-BE-064`, FR-019).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** Neither roles, nor an ownership predicate, nor 401/403 behaviour for `API-SEARCH-580505` can be written from the inputs.\n\n**What would unblock it:**\n- `Q-BE-002`: note ownership (per user, or shared team workspace) and the access rule per note and search operation. BR-031 is a placeholder.\n- `Q-BE-003`: the session model, including whether search requires a session.\n- `Q-BE-001`: which addresses may obtain a login code (BR-030, garbled).\n\n**What is not asserted.**\n- Whether a caller sees all notes or only their own.\n- Whether an unauthenticated call is refused.\n- Any role or permission matrix entry.\n\nUS-004-03 requires 401 and 403 to be documented for endpoints that require authentication. Whether this endpoint is one of them is part of the same open question.\n\nThe working premise is `ASM-BE-071`, nothing more.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: UNKNOWN -->\n## Jobs\n\n**No scheduled job, sweep or retry job is derived from the inputs for this module.**\n- Retention purge belongs to `auth` (NFR-019).\n- Residency scans belong to `residency`.\n\n**Idempotency.** `API-SEARCH-580505` is a read-only GET (`INV-BE-051`), so it is idempotent and needs no idempotency key.\n\n**Retry.** After a 503 the client retries per the retry-after header (FR-017). The server runs no retry.\n\n**Index maintenance.** How the index is built, rebuilt and kept current is unstated (`GAP-BE-075`). `ASM-BE-068` assumes it is updated with note writes, so no job is needed.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\nAll test names are proposed (planned).\n\n**Unit**\n- Parameter parsing and validation, covering `q`, `tags`, `include_archived`, `page` and `limit`.\n  - Tests: `parse_q_trims_and_treats_blank_as_absent`, `parse_tags_splits_and_rejects_invalid`, `parse_include_archived_accepts_only_true_false`, `parse_paging_rejects_negative_zero_nonnumeric_and_over_max`.\n  - Covers FR-003, 006, 009, 012, 016.\n- Ordering comparator.\n  - Test: `order_pinned_then_relevance_then_id`.\n  - Covers FR-019.\n\n**Integration** (database and index in place)\n- Keyword match across title and body, and no match.\n  - Tests: `search_keyword_matches_title_or_body`, `search_no_match_returns_empty_200`.\n  - Covers FR-001, 002.\n- Tag filter and combined filters, including the empty cases.\n  - Tests: `search_by_tag_returns_only_tagged`, `search_unused_tag_returns_empty_200`, `search_q_and_tags_requires_both`, `search_combined_no_overlap_returns_empty`.\n  - Covers FR-004, 005, 007, 008.\n- Archived exclusion and `include_archived=true`.\n  - Tests: `search_default_excludes_archived`, `search_include_archived_true_includes_archived`.\n  - Covers FR-014, 015.\n- Pagination, total count, and a page beyond the results.\n  - Tests: `search_pagination_returns_subset_and_total`, `search_page_beyond_results_returns_empty_200`, `search_default_and_max_page_size_enforced`.\n  - Covers FR-010, 011, 013.\n- Pinned-first ordering across pages and stable order.\n  - Tests: `search_pinned_first_across_pages`, `search_order_is_stable_between_pages`.\n  - Covers FR-019 and INV-BE-053, INV-BE-057.\n- Route precedence.\n  - Test: `search_route_not_shadowed_by_note_get`.\n  - Covers FR-020.\n- Read-only behaviour.\n  - Test: `search_leaves_all_tables_unchanged`.\n  - Covers INV-BE-051.\n- Degraded index.\n  - Tests: `search_index_degraded_returns_503_with_retry_after`, `search_invalid_params_win_over_degraded_503`.\n  - Covers FR-017, `ASM-BE-072`.\n\n**Contract**\n- `API-SEARCH-580505` appears in the published OpenAPI document with 200, 400 and 503 documented (BR-012, NFR-008).\n  - Test: `openapi_documents_search_operation_with_200_400_503`.\n- Example payloads exist for search, including an empty-result example (NFR-009, US-004-05).\n  - Test: `openapi_search_has_examples_including_empty_result`.\n- The spec-versus-route coverage check reports no undocumented search route.\n  - Test: `coverage_check_includes_search_route`.\n- 401/403 documentation is deferred until `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003` are answered.\n\n**Non-functional**\n- Load test: p95 under 300 ms at 50 requests per second on about 100k notes, including `limit=100`.\n  - Test: `search_p95_under_300ms_at_50rps_100k_notes`.\n  - Covers FR-018, NFR-006, NFR-007.\n- Injection suite and the per-release security scan (FR-021, NFR-012).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions, questions and assumptions\n\n### Gaps minted by this module\n\n- **`GAP-BE-073`: \"relevance\" is undefined.**\n  - No input defines the scoring or the order for a tag-only search after pinned notes.\n  - Degrades FR-001 and FR-019.\n  - Fallback: `ASM-BE-064`.\n  - Closing artefact: a ranking rule.\n- **`GAP-BE-074`: keyword matching semantics are unstated.**\n  - Missing: case, partial versus whole-word, stemming, language, multi-word handling and maximum `q` length.\n  - Degrades FR-001.\n  - Fallback: `ASM-BE-063`.\n  - Closing artefact: a matching rule.\n- **`GAP-BE-075`: index behaviour is unstated.**\n  - Missing: technology, type, how it is kept current, what \"degraded\" means, how degradation is detected and cleared, and whether a dedicated (pinned, status) index is needed.\n  - Degrades `backend.search.data-model`, `backend.search.state-machine` and FR-017.\n  - Fallback: `ASM-BE-068`, `ASM-BE-069`.\n  - Closing artefact: an index design statement.\n- **`GAP-BE-076`: multi-tag semantics and the `tags` encoding are unstated.**\n  - Missing: all-of versus any-of, and comma-separated versus repeated parameter.\n  - Degrades FR-004 and FR-007.\n  - Fallback: `ASM-BE-061`, `ASM-BE-062`.\n  - Closing artefact: a filter rule.\n- **`GAP-BE-077`: \"malformed filter combination\" is undefined.**\n  - US-003-04 requires a 400 but no input says what is malformed beyond the other validation rules.\n  - Degrades FR-009.\n  - Closing artefact: a list of invalid combinations.\n- **`GAP-BE-078`: no default page size is stated.** The maximum is only implied by \"up to page sizes of 100\" (NFR-007).\n  - Degrades FR-013.\n  - Fallback: `ASM-BE-065`.\n  - Closing artefact: a paging statement.\n- **`GAP-BE-079`: handling of search terms in logs is unstated.**\n  - NFR-018 covers emails and login codes only. Whether note content in `q` is logged or redacted is not stated, and no redaction is asserted.\n  - Closing artefact: a logging statement.\n- **`GAP-BE-080`: the rate-limit class for search is unstated.**\n  - Closing artefact: an NFR statement.\n\n### Referenced, not minted\n\n- `GAP-BE-002`: no list-notes operation, including an archived-only view.\n- `GAP-BE-003`: tag rules.\n- `GAP-BE-004`: error envelope, code names and retry values.\n- `CON-BE-002`: US-003-01 returns 400 when `q` is missing, but US-003-02 and BR-010 allow tags-only searches. The tags-only reading is used (`ASM-BE-004`).\n- `ASM-BE-004`, `ASM-BE-006`.\n- Blocking questions `Q-BE-002`, `Q-BE-003`, plus `Q-BE-001`.\n\nNo `CON-` is minted here.\n\nDirections checked:\n- story \u2194 design: no design content (`design-brief.md` repeats the acceptance criteria).\n- story \u2194 requirement: consistent apart from `CON-BE-002`.\n- requirement \u2194 requirement: consistent within this module.\n- design \u2194 design: not applicable.\n\n### Questions (all degradable)\n\nUnattended defaults apply.\n\n- **`Q-BE-061`:** how should keywords match, and how are results ranked? Default: `ASM-BE-063`, `ASM-BE-064`.\n- **`Q-BE-062`:** with several tags, must a note carry all of them or any? How is the `tags` parameter encoded? Default: `ASM-BE-061`, `ASM-BE-062`.\n- **`Q-BE-063`:** what are the default and maximum page sizes, and is an oversize `limit` rejected or clamped? Default: `ASM-BE-065`.\n- **`Q-BE-064`:** what does \"degraded\" mean, and must a note be searchable as soon as its write returns? Default: `ASM-BE-068`, `ASM-BE-069`.\n\n### Assumptions\n\nEach is confirmed by the answer to the named question or artefact.\n\n- **`ASM-BE-061`:** the `tags` parameter is one comma-separated value, such as `tags=a,b`.\n  - Reasoning: US-003-02 shows the single form `tags=project-x` and speaks of \"one or more tags\".\n  - Confirmed by `Q-BE-062`.\n- **`ASM-BE-062`:** with several tags, a note must carry all of them. This is consistent with the combined-filter wording in US-004-04.\n  - Confirmed by `Q-BE-062`.\n- **`ASM-BE-063`:** `q` is trimmed. A whitespace-only `q` counts as absent. The match is case-insensitive over title and body.\n  - Whether it is partial or whole-word, and whether stemming applies, is left to `Q-BE-061`.\n- **`ASM-BE-064`:** within pinned and unpinned groups, results are ordered by relevance when `q` is given, then by note id. For a tag-only search, order is by note id after pinned notes.\n  - Reasoning: deterministic paging requires a total order. BR-019 only says same-pinned ordering is \"unchanged\".\n  - Confirmed by `Q-BE-061`.\n- **`ASM-BE-065`:** `page` is 1-based and defaults to 1. `page=0` and `limit=0` return 400. The maximum `limit` is 100, from the NFR-007 wording. A larger `limit` returns 400 and is not clamped.\n  - **The default `limit` is 20. This figure has no source** and is a placeholder.\n  - Confirmed by `Q-BE-063`.\n- **`ASM-BE-066`:** `include_archived` accepts exactly `true` and `false`, in lowercase. Absent means `false`.\n  - Reasoning: US-003-06 names only `true` and \"invalid\".\n  - Confirmed by a statement of accepted values.\n- **`ASM-BE-067`:** the total count is the number of notes matching the request across all pages. It is still reported on a page beyond the results.\n  - Confirmed by the response schema decision.\n- **`ASM-BE-068`:** the search index lives in the PostgreSQL 16 database (the only store `platform.md` names). It is updated with note writes, so a note is searchable once its write returns.\n  - Reasoning: web_app saves and then searches (US-006-02, US-006-03).\n  - Confirmed by `Q-BE-064`.\n- **`ASM-BE-069`:** \"degraded\" means the index cannot answer the query. 503 carries a `Retry-After` header, whose value is `Q-BE-004`.\n- **`ASM-BE-070`:** `/notes/search` is registered before the parameterised note-get route and takes precedence over it.\n  - Reasoning: Express matches routes in registration order (`platform.md` names Express). Whether a note id can equal `search` is unstated.\n  - Confirmed by the note id format.\n- **`ASM-BE-071`:** until `Q-BE-002` is answered, search is specified without any per-user scoping.\n  - Reasoning: BR-031's rationale calls the shared-workspace default only a placeholder.\n  - The authorization section remains BLOCKED.\n- **`ASM-BE-072`:** all parameters are validated before the index is consulted. An invalid request gets 400 even when the index is degraded.\n  - Several validation errors are reported in one 400. Their shape is `GAP-BE-004`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: auth\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-001, Q-BE-003]\nscope: [API-AUTH-015365, API-AUTH-592570]\n```\n\nDerivation:\n- `scope` lists the two operation ids minted by DevAgent for this module.\n- `blocked_by` names the two blocking questions that touch this module (see `backend.auth.gaps`).\n  - `Q-BE-001`: who may request and receive a code (BR-030).\n  - `Q-BE-003`: what a successful verification yields (session model).\n- `Q-BE-002` (note ownership) does not block this module.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsible for** (module map: `backend.module-map`; US-006-06; BR-020 to BR-033; NFR-015 to NFR-019):\n- Issuing a 6-digit numeric one-time login code for an email address (BR-029) and emailing it (NFR-017).\n- Expiring each code 10 minutes after issue (BR-020, BR-024, BR-026).\n- Limiting code requests to 3 within 10 minutes per email, answering 429 beyond that (BR-021, BR-028, BR-032).\n- Verifying a submitted code. A code is single use (BR-023). Wrong, expired and already-used codes are told apart by the status mapping in BR-033.\n- Locking verification for 15 minutes after 5 wrong codes (BR-022, BR-025, BR-027). During a lock only verification is refused; requesting a code is not (BR-032).\n- Keeping code challenges, send attempts and account locks, and purging them after their retention period (NFR-019, read per `ASM-BE-007`).\n- Keeping codes and unmasked email local parts out of logs (NFR-018).\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n- **The credential or session issued after a successful verification.** No input defines it (`Q-BE-003`). This module records the verification outcome only.\n- **Who is eligible to request or receive a code.** BR-030 is garbled and open (`Q-BE-001`). `ASM-BE-111` is the working default.\n- **Note ownership and per-note access rules** (BR-031). These belong to `notes` and `search` and are pending `Q-BE-002`.\n- **Existing login methods.** None is described by any input (`GAP-BE-008`).\n- **The web_app login and code-entry screens.** They belong to a separate surface (US-006-06).\n- **Email delivery infrastructure.** It is an external service in AWS eu-west-1 (NFR-017).\n- **Publishing the OpenAPI document and the coverage check.** `contract-docs` owns these.\n- **Refusing out-of-scope calls.** `scope-guard` owns this.\n- **Residency scanning.** `residency` owns it.\n- **Defining schemas, error envelopes or error-code names.** Those live in `backend.error-model` and the operation records, and are referenced here.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\nTable names, column names and key types are proposals (`ASM-BE-119`). The records themselves are the three named by NFR-019. The database is PostgreSQL 16 (`platform.md`). Every timestamp is `timestamptz` and stored in UTC. The email key is normalised per `ASM-BE-116`.\n\n### `login_code_challenges`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | primary key |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | normalised address; PII |\n| `code_secret` | text | NOT NULL | none | the code, 6 numeric digits (BR-029). Storage form unresolved (`GAP-BE-012`); the column is a placeholder. |\n| `issued_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n| `expires_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | `issued_at` + 10 minutes, set at issue (BR-020, BR-024) |\n| `state` | text | NOT NULL | `'ACTIVE'` | one of `ACTIVE`, `USED`, `SUPERSEDED` |\n| `used_at` | timestamptz | NULL | NULL | non-null if and only if `state = 'USED'` |\n\nConstraints:\n- `CHECK (expires_at > issued_at)`.\n- `CHECK (state IN ('ACTIVE','USED','SUPERSEDED'))`.\n- `CHECK ((state = 'USED') = (used_at IS NOT NULL))`.\n- `CHECK (code_secret` is exactly 6 numeric digits`)`, enforced at the application layer if `code_secret` is stored in a non-readable form (`GAP-BE-012`).\n- Partial unique index on `(email) WHERE state = 'ACTIVE'`. It enforces one active code per email (`INV-BE-108`).\n\nIndexes:\n- `(email, issued_at DESC)`. It serves the lookup of the latest challenges for an email on verify.\n- `(issued_at)`. It serves the retention sweep.\n\n### `login_send_attempts`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | generated | primary key |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | normalised; PII |\n| `requested_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n| `challenge_id` | uuid | NULL | NULL | FK to `login_code_challenges(id)`, `ON DELETE SET NULL` |\n\n- Only accepted requests are recorded (`ASM-BE-112`).\n- Index `(email, requested_at)`. It serves the count of accepted requests in the last 10 minutes.\n- Index `(requested_at)`. It serves the retention sweep.\n\n### `account_locks`\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key; normalised; PII |\n| `wrong_code_count` | smallint | NOT NULL | 0 | `CHECK (wrong_code_count BETWEEN 0 AND 5)` |\n| `locked_until` | timestamptz | NULL | NULL | non-null while a lock has been set and not yet reset |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | set on every change |\n\n- `CHECK (wrong_code_count < 5 OR locked_until IS NOT NULL)` (`INV-BE-104`).\n- A row is created on the first wrong code for an email. An absent row means count 0 and not locked.\n- Index `(updated_at)`. It serves the retention sweep.\n- The primary key serves the per-request lookup.\n\n### Retention\n\nPer `ASM-BE-007`: challenges 1 day from `issued_at`, send attempts 1 day from `requested_at`, locks 5 days from `updated_at`. The sweep is specified in `backend.auth.jobs`.\n\n### PII and sensitivity\n\n- `email` is PII in all three tables.\n- `code_secret` is a credential-grade value.\n- At-rest treatment of both is unstated (`GAP-BE-006`) and not asserted here.\n- Every example uses a masked address such as `j***@example.com`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\n### Challenge (`login_code_challenges.state`)\n\nStates: `ACTIVE`, `USED`, `SUPERSEDED`. Expiry is derived, not stored: an `ACTIVE` challenge with `now >= expires_at` is *expired* (`ASM-BE-005`).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (none) | `ACTIVE` | accepted `API-AUTH-015365` | valid email; fewer than 3 accepted requests for the email in the last 10 minutes; the email was handed to the delivery service (`ASM-BE-117`) |\n| `ACTIVE` | `SUPERSEDED` | a later accepted `API-AUTH-015365` for the same email | same transaction as the new challenge's creation (`ASM-BE-113`) |\n| `ACTIVE` | `USED` | successful `API-AUTH-592570` | email not locked; submitted code matches; `now < expires_at` |\n\n**Terminal states:**\n- `USED` and `SUPERSEDED` are terminal.\n- An `ACTIVE` challenge past `expires_at` can never become `USED`, so it is effectively terminal (`INV-BE-107`).\n\n**Illegal or non-transitioning submissions** (no challenge changes state):\n\n| Submission | Outcome | Basis |\n|---|---|---|\n| matches an expired `ACTIVE` challenge | 410 | BR-033 |\n| matches the most recent `USED` challenge and matches no `ACTIVE` one | 409 | BR-023, BR-033 |\n| matches nothing, or matches only a `SUPERSEDED` one | 401, counted as wrong | BR-033, `ASM-BE-118` |\n\n### Lock (per email, `account_locks`)\n\nStates: `OPEN` (count 0 to 4, `locked_until` unset or past) and `LOCKED` (`locked_until` in the future).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `OPEN` | `OPEN` | wrong code (401) | new count < 5 |\n| `OPEN` | `LOCKED` | wrong code | new count reaches 5. `locked_until = now + 15 minutes`; this fifth response is still 401 (`ASM-BE-115`) |\n| `OPEN` | `OPEN` (count 0) | successful verify | |\n| `LOCKED` | `LOCKED` | any `API-AUTH-592570` | `now < locked_until`; answered 423, nothing changes |\n| `LOCKED` | `OPEN` (count 0, `locked_until` cleared) | next verify or the sweep | `now >= locked_until` (`ASM-BE-114`) |\n\n- Neither lock state is terminal.\n- `API-AUTH-015365` neither reads nor changes this state (BR-032, `INV-BE-109`).\n- Expired-code and used-code submissions do not move the count (`ASM-BE-008`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nError bodies, envelope and machine-readable code names come from `backend.error-model`. They are not restated here. Retry hints and remaining lock time are open (`GAP-BE-004`).\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-001. Issue a code.**\n- Given a valid email with fewer than 3 accepted requests in the last 10 minutes,\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called,\n- Then a 6-digit all-numeric code (leading zeros preserved) is generated,\n  - a challenge is stored `ACTIVE` with `expires_at = issued_at + 10 minutes`,\n  - any earlier `ACTIVE` challenge for that email becomes `SUPERSEDED`,\n  - one send attempt is recorded,\n  - the code is emailed,\n  - and the success status is the one in the operation record.\n- Traces to. US-006-06; BR-020, BR-024, BR-026, BR-029; NFR-015.\n- Verification. `auth.request.issues_six_digit_code` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-002. Resend limit.**\n- Given an email with 3 accepted requests in the last 10 minutes (rolling window, `ASM-BE-004`),\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called again,\n- Then the response is 429,\n  - no challenge is created,\n  - nothing is emailed,\n  - and nothing is recorded (`ASM-BE-112`).\n- Traces to. US-006-06 (first criterion); BR-021, BR-028, BR-032.\n- Verification. `auth.request.fourth_request_in_window_returns_429` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-003. Request during a lock.**\n- Given an email whose verification is locked and which is under the resend limit,\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called,\n- Then it is processed as in FR-001,\n  - and the lock record is unchanged.\n- Traces to. BR-032.\n- Verification. `auth.request.allowed_while_locked` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-004. Request validation.**\n- Given a missing, empty or invalid email (validity rules: `GAP-BE-124`),\n- When `API-AUTH-015365` is called,\n- Then the response is 400,\n  - and no challenge or attempt is recorded.\n- Traces to. US-004-03 (400 documented for validation errors); US-006-06.\n- Verification. `auth.request.invalid_email_returns_400` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-005. Delivery.**\n- Given an accepted request,\n- When the code email is sent,\n- Then it goes through the transactional email service in AWS eu-west-1 (NFR-017).\n- Given the service fails to accept the email,\n- When the request is processed,\n- Then the response is 500,\n  - no `ACTIVE` challenge from this call remains,\n  - no attempt is recorded (`ASM-BE-117`),\n  - and no automatic retry is made.\n- The clause of NFR-017 on transfers outside the EU is garbled and is not asserted (`GAP-BE-005`).\n- Traces to. NFR-017; US-006-06.\n- Verification. `auth.request.delivery_failure_leaves_no_active_challenge` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-006. Verify a correct code.**\n- Given an email that is not locked and has an `ACTIVE` challenge with `now < expires_at`,\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called with the matching code,\n- Then the challenge becomes `USED` with `used_at` set,\n  - the wrong-code count resets to 0,\n  - and the success status is the one in the operation record.\n- What credential accompanies success is undecided (`Q-BE-003`). It is not specified here.\n- Traces to. US-006-06; BR-020, BR-023.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.correct_code_consumes_challenge` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-007. Wrong code.**\n- Given an email that is not locked,\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called with a well-formed code that matches no `ACTIVE` challenge and no most-recent `USED` challenge for that email (`ASM-BE-118`),\n- Then the response is 401,\n  - and the wrong-code count increases by 1.\n- When the count reaches 5, `locked_until` is set to now plus 15 minutes. That fifth response is still 401 (`ASM-BE-115`).\n- An email with no records is counted the same way (`ASM-BE-118`).\n- Traces to. US-006-06 (third criterion); BR-022, BR-025, BR-027, BR-033.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.fifth_wrong_code_locks_for_15_minutes` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-008. Locked.**\n- Given `locked_until > now` for the email,\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called with any code, including a correct one,\n- Then the response is 423,\n  - the count is unchanged,\n  - and no challenge is consumed.\n- Traces to. US-006-06 (third criterion); BR-022, BR-032, BR-033.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.locked_refuses_even_correct_code` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-009. Expired.**\n- Given an email that is not locked and an `ACTIVE` challenge with `now >= expires_at` (a code at exactly 10 minutes is expired, `ASM-BE-005`),\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called with the matching code,\n- Then the response is 410,\n  - the challenge is not consumed,\n  - and the count is unchanged (`ASM-BE-008`).\n- Traces to. US-006-06 (second criterion); BR-020, BR-024, BR-026, BR-033.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.expired_code_returns_410_at_boundary` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-010. Already used.**\n- Given an email that is not locked, a code that matches the most recent `USED` challenge, and no matching `ACTIVE` challenge (`ASM-BE-118`),\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called,\n- Then the response is 409,\n  - and the count is unchanged (`ASM-BE-008`).\n- Traces to. BR-023, BR-033.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.reused_code_returns_409` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-011. Lock expiry.**\n- Given `locked_until <= now` (the exact instant counts as unlocked),\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called,\n- Then the lock is cleared and the count reset to 0 before the code is evaluated (`ASM-BE-114`),\n  - and the submission is then handled by FR-006 to FR-010.\n- Traces to. BR-022, BR-025.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.lock_clears_after_15_minutes` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-012. Verify validation.**\n- Given a missing email, a missing code, or a code that is not exactly 6 numeric digits,\n- When `API-AUTH-592570` is called,\n- Then the response is 400,\n  - and the count is unchanged (`ASM-BE-120`).\n- Traces to. BR-029; US-004-03.\n- Verification. `auth.verify.malformed_input_returns_400_uncounted` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-013. Concurrency.**\n- Given concurrent calls for the same email,\n- When they are processed,\n- Then each operation runs serialised per email in a transaction (`ASM-BE-121`),\n  - of two simultaneous correct verifications exactly one succeeds (BR-023),\n  - every concurrent wrong code is counted,\n  - and the accepted-request count never exceeds 3 in a window (`INV-BE-103`).\n- Traces to. BR-021, BR-022, BR-023.\n- Verification. `auth.concurrency.single_use_and_limit_hold` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`, `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-014. Logging.**\n- Given any call to either operation,\n- When the module logs,\n- Then no code value appears in any log entry,\n  - and any email in a log has its local part masked.\n- Traces to. NFR-018.\n- Verification. `auth.logging.no_codes_no_unmasked_email` (planned).\n- API. `API-AUTH-015365`, `API-AUTH-592570`.\n\n**BE-AUTH-FR-015. Retention.**\n- Given a record older than its retention period (`ASM-BE-007`),\n- When the sweep runs,\n- Then the record is deleted.\n- Traces to. NFR-019.\n- Verification. `auth.jobs.sweep_deletes_expired_records` (planned).\n- API. none.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-AUTH-015365` | POST | `/auth/request-code` | `auth` | 202 | 400, 429, 500 | Request an email one-time login code: 6 digits, expires 10 minutes after issue, limited to 3 requests per 10 minutes per email (429 beyond that). Allowed even while verification is locked (BR-032). |\n| `API-AUTH-592570` | POST | `/auth/verify-code` | `auth` | 200 | 400, 401, 409, 410, 423, 500 | Verify an emailed one-time code. Errors: wrong code 401, already used 409, expired 410, locked after 5 wrong codes for 15 minutes 423 (BR-033). |\n\n### `API-AUTH-015365` \u2014 POST `/auth/request-code`\n\nRequest an email one-time login code: 6 digits, expires 10 minutes after issue, limited to 3 requests per 10 minutes per email (429 beyond that). Allowed even while verification is locked (BR-032).\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address that will receive the 6-digit one-time login code. PII: masked in logs and examples.\n**Response:** \n- `message` (string): Confirmation that the code request was accepted and a code email is being sent. Does not contain the code.\n\n### `API-AUTH-592570` \u2014 POST `/auth/verify-code`\n\nVerify an emailed one-time code. Errors: wrong code 401, already used 409, expired 410, locked after 5 wrong codes for 15 minutes 423 (BR-033).\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address the code was issued to. PII: masked in logs and examples.\n- `code` (string, required): The 6-digit numeric one-time code received by email. Single use; never logged.\n**Response:** \n- `message` (string): Confirmation that the code was valid and login succeeded. The session credential (type, lifetime, transport) is undecided pending Q-BE-003, so no credential field is asserted.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-101.** A challenge reaches `USED` at most once, and a `USED` challenge is never consumed again (BR-023).\n- **INV-BE-102.** No verification succeeds while the email's `locked_until` is in the future (BR-022).\n- **INV-BE-103.** For any email, accepted send attempts in any rolling 10-minute window number at most 3 (BR-021, BR-028).\n- **INV-BE-104.** `wrong_code_count` is between 0 and 5. A count of 5 always has a non-null `locked_until`, set to 15 minutes after the fifth wrong code (BR-022, BR-025).\n- **INV-BE-105.** No log entry contains a code value or an unmasked email local part (NFR-018).\n- **INV-BE-106.** Every issued code is exactly 6 numeric digits (BR-029).\n- **INV-BE-107.** No challenge becomes `USED` at or after its `expires_at` (BR-020, `ASM-BE-005`).\n- **INV-BE-108.** At most one `ACTIVE` challenge exists per email (`ASM-BE-113`).\n- **INV-BE-109.** `API-AUTH-015365` never reads or modifies `account_locks` (BR-032).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** The roles, the permission matrix and the ownership predicate for these operations cannot be written.\n\nWhat would unblock it:\n1. `Q-BE-001`. BR-030 is garbled and open. Which email addresses may request, verify and receive a code? Until it is answered, `ASM-BE-111` is only a working default and no permission rule is asserted.\n2. `Q-BE-003`. The session model is undecided. No input says whether either operation requires an existing session, nor what a successful verification grants.\n3. The project-level `backend.auth-model` is itself `BLOCKED` on the same questions. This section follows it once it is unblocked.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n### Retention sweep\n\n- **Purpose.** Meets NFR-019 and supports `BE-AUTH-FR-015`.\n- **Work.**\n  - Delete `login_code_challenges` where `issued_at` is older than 1 day.\n  - Delete `login_send_attempts` where `requested_at` is older than 1 day.\n  - Delete `account_locks` where `updated_at` is older than 5 days.\n  - Periods are per `ASM-BE-007`.\n- **Guard.** A lock row with `locked_until > now` is never deleted. A challenge that is `ACTIVE` and unexpired is never deleted.\n- **Idempotency.** It is delete-by-predicate, so a re-run changes nothing further.\n- **Cadence and maximum deletion lag.** Unstated (`GAP-BE-123`). No value is invented.\n- **Retries.** A failed sweep is re-run on the next scheduled run. No retry policy is stated by any input.\n\n### Lock expiry\n\nThere is no job. A lock clears lazily on the next verify (`BE-AUTH-FR-011`) or when the sweep removes the row.\n\n### Idempotency of the operations\n\n- `API-AUTH-015365` is deliberately not idempotent: each accepted call issues a new code and counts toward the limit of 3.\n- `API-AUTH-592570` repeated with the same correct code succeeds once, then returns 409 (BR-023).\n- No client-supplied idempotency key is defined by any input, and none is introduced.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Test plan\n\nThe test framework and the email service identity are unnamed by the inputs (`GAP-BE-125`). Plans name behaviours, not tools. Time-dependent behaviour assumes an injectable clock.\n\n### Unit\n\n- Code generation: always 6 numeric digits, leading zeros preserved (`BE-AUTH-FR-001`, `INV-BE-106`).\n- Expiry boundary: 9m59s accepted, exactly 10m expired (`BE-AUTH-FR-009`, `INV-BE-107`).\n- Rolling-window counter: the 4th request inside 10 minutes is refused, and one made after the window slides is accepted (`BE-AUTH-FR-002`).\n- Lock counter: increments on a wrong code only, locks at 5, the fifth response stays 401, resets on success and at `locked_until` (`BE-AUTH-FR-007`, `BE-AUTH-FR-011`).\n- Match order: `ACTIVE` before `USED`, superseded and unknown codes are wrong (`ASM-BE-118`).\n- Email normalisation and masking (`ASM-BE-116`, `BE-AUTH-FR-014`).\n\n### Integration (against PostgreSQL 16)\n\n- Full flow: request, verify, then verify again returns 409 (`BE-AUTH-FR-006`, `BE-AUTH-FR-010`).\n- The 5-wrong-code lock, a correct code during the lock returning 423, request-code still accepted during the lock, and recovery after 15 minutes (`BE-AUTH-FR-003`, `BE-AUTH-FR-008`, `BE-AUTH-FR-011`).\n- A second accepted request supersedes the first challenge, with a single `ACTIVE` row enforced by the partial unique index (`INV-BE-108`).\n- Delivery failure leaves no `ACTIVE` challenge and no attempt row (`BE-AUTH-FR-005`).\n- Concurrency: simultaneous correct verifications give exactly one success, and simultaneous requests never exceed 3 (`BE-AUTH-FR-013`).\n- Retention sweep deletes only aged rows and never an active lock (`BE-AUTH-FR-015`).\n- Log capture shows no code and no unmasked email (`BE-AUTH-FR-014`, `INV-BE-105`).\n\n### Contract\n\n- `contracts/openapi.yaml` documents, for `API-AUTH-015365`: 429 and 400, plus the error responses in its record.\n- It documents, for `API-AUTH-592570`: 401, 409, 410, 423 and 400 (BR-033, US-004-03).\n- The status each FR returns matches the documented status for that operation.\n- Neither operation appears in the out-of-scope categories checked by `scope-guard`.\n- Spec-versus-route coverage for these two routes is owned by `contract-docs` (NFR-008).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n**Checks run.** Story to requirement, requirement to requirement, story to design (the design brief repeats acceptance criteria and adds nothing for this module) and design to design. The garbled text of BR-027, BR-030, BR-031, BR-033 and NFR-017 to NFR-019 is a gap (`GAP-BE-005`), not a contradiction. No new `CON-` is raised. `CON-BE-001` (CR record versus BR-021, BR-028, BR-032 on resend) applies, and the requirements values are used.\n\n**No new `Q-` is minted.** The open questions are `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003` (both blocking this module), `Q-BE-004` and `Q-BE-009`. Defaults for the degradable ones are in `ASM-BE-115` and `ASM-BE-008`.\n\n### New gaps\n\n- **GAP-BE-123.** The retention sweep has no cadence or maximum deletion lag. NFR-019's target is the share of aged records deleted, and the \"age from\" basis for each table is `ASM-BE-007` plus `ASM-BE-122`. Degrades `backend.auth.jobs`. Closing artefact: an NFR statement giving the sweep cadence and permitted lag.\n- **GAP-BE-124.** The validity rules for an email address are not stated: format, maximum length, case sensitivity. Degrades `BE-AUTH-FR-004`. Closing artefact: a requirement stating the accepted email form.\n- **GAP-BE-125.** The transactional email service is unnamed, and so are its failure modes, its send retries and the test framework. Degrades `BE-AUTH-FR-005` and the test plan. Closing artefact: a platform statement naming the email service and test tooling.\n\n### Open items cited from the project spec\n\n- `GAP-BE-001`: the four web-app handoff files were not supplied.\n  - `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-752470` (session model) cannot be answered, because it depends on `Q-BE-003`.\n  - `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-733353` (retry-after values, remaining lock time, re-enable rule, 400 detail) cannot be answered, because it depends on `GAP-BE-004`.\n  - Two points are answered from requirements alone: requesting a code is allowed during a lock (BR-032), and the lock status is 423 (BR-033).\n- `GAP-BE-004`: retry values, remaining lock time and the envelope.\n- `GAP-BE-005`: garbled requirement text.\n- `GAP-BE-006`: encryption at rest and in transit.\n- `GAP-BE-007`: no performance targets for the login operations.\n- `GAP-BE-008`: existing login methods unspecified.\n- `GAP-BE-012`: how a code is stored.\n\n### Assumptions raised by this module\n\n- **ASM-BE-111.** Any syntactically valid email may request, verify and receive a code. Confirms with `Q-BE-001`.\n- **ASM-BE-112.** A request refused with 429 is not recorded. Only accepted requests count toward the 3-per-10-minutes limit.\n- **ASM-BE-113.** An accepted request supersedes any earlier `ACTIVE` challenge for the same email. This is not stated by BR-021 or BR-028, and the alternative is that every unexpired code stays valid.\n- **ASM-BE-114.** The wrong-code count resets on a successful verification and when the lock has expired, with no time decay. A lock is expired at exactly `locked_until`. While locked the count stays at 5. Refines `ASM-BE-002`.\n- **ASM-BE-115.** The fifth wrong code returns 401 and locks. The next attempt returns 423. Default for the degradable boundary question.\n- **ASM-BE-116.** Email is trimmed and lower-cased for keying and comparison. Refines `ASM-BE-001`.\n- **ASM-BE-117.** A delivery failure returns 500, leaves no `ACTIVE` challenge from that call and no recorded attempt, and is not retried.\n- **ASM-BE-118.** A submitted code is matched against the `ACTIVE` challenge first, then the most recent `USED` challenge. A superseded or unknown code is wrong (401), and an email with no records still counts toward the lock. Because the match runs against the latest `ACTIVE` challenge, a previous expired code replaced by a new request reads as wrong rather than expired.\n- **ASM-BE-119.** Table names, column names and key types are proposals, because no input names them.\n- **ASM-BE-120.** A code that is not exactly 6 numeric digits is a 400 validation failure and is not counted as a wrong code.\n- **ASM-BE-121.** Operations are serialised per email inside a transaction. The mechanism is not specified.\n- **ASM-BE-122.** Retention age is measured from `issued_at` (challenges), `requested_at` (attempts) and `updated_at` (locks). Refines `ASM-BE-007`.\n\n### Assumptions cited from the project spec\n\n`ASM-BE-001`, `ASM-BE-002`, `ASM-BE-004`, `ASM-BE-005`, `ASM-BE-006`, `ASM-BE-007`, `ASM-BE-008`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-006-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/contract-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/contract-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.frontmatter | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: contract-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: [API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363, API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943, API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650, API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `contract-docs` publishes the machine-readable description of the API and measures how complete that description is. Source: `backend.module-map`; US-004-01 to US-004-06, US-005-01; BR-012, BR-013; NFR-008, NFR-009.\n\nIt is responsible for:\n- The published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint (US-004-01, BR-012), served by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`.\n- The spec-versus-route coverage check and its result (US-004-04, NFR-008), served by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`.\n- The data-residency note (US-004-06, BR-013), served by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650` and carried in the document.\n- The Scope section stating what the API does not cover (US-005-01), served by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969` and carried in the document.\n- The CI checks that fail a build when the spec and the code diverge, when the spec is malformed, or when the Scope section is missing (US-004-02, US-004-04, US-005-01).\n\n**Not responsible for:**\n- Defining any endpoint, request or response shape, status code or error code. Those belong to the owning module and to `backend.error-model`. This module renders what they define and restates nothing (core rule 6).\n- Enforcing the exclusion of attachment, real-time and mobile endpoints, or refusing calls to them. That belongs to `scope-guard`. This module only documents the exclusion.\n- Deciding the data-residency facts. They belong to `backend.data-lifecycle` and `residency`. This module publishes them.\n- The review and approval workflow for documentation changes (US-005-01, second criterion). This is a repository process, not a runtime capability (`GAP-BE-179`).\n- Product-manager request logging (BR-017). It has no backend owner (`GAP-BE-009`).\n- Any UI. The consuming web app is a separate surface.\n\n**Inbound handoffs.** The four web-app handoffs are listed in `RUN-CONTEXT.upstream.handoffs`. Their bodies were not supplied (`GAP-BE-001`). `feedback.md` summarises them. The summary of `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-733353` (retry-after values, remaining lock time, the 400 field-detail shape) touches the error documentation this module renders. It cannot be answered here, because the values are decided in `backend.error-model` and not supplied (`GAP-BE-004`). The other three concern modules this one does not own. That reading rests on `ASM-BE-010`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Data model\n\n**This module owns no tables, columns or indexes** (`ASM-BE-161`). Every artefact it works with is derived at build or request time from the route registry and the module specifications. Nothing in the inputs requires persisting any of them.\n\n| Logical artefact | Derived from | Persisted | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Route registry | The implemented routes of the running service | No, it is the mounted route set | BR-012 (enforcement: \"route registry\") |\n| Published OpenAPI document | The route registry and the owning modules' operation records | No (`ASM-BE-161`, `ASM-BE-169`) | US-004-01, BR-012 |\n| Data-residency note | One source text shared by the document and `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650` (`ASM-BE-166`) | No | BR-013, US-004-06 |\n| Scope section | One source text shared by the document and `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969` (`ASM-BE-166`) | No | US-005-01 |\n| Coverage result | A comparison of registry and document | No, computed per call or per build | US-004-04 |\n\n**Schemas.** No `SCHEMA-` id is minted by this module. The shapes of the four operations' bodies are rendered into `backend.contract-docs.api-contract` from the operation records.\n\n**Retention.** No retention applies, because nothing is stored. The history of coverage results over time is not specified by any input (`GAP-BE-177`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## State machine\n\nNo entity has a status column. The only lifecycle here is that of one documentation build. It is derived from the checks that US-004-02, US-004-04 and US-005-01 require to fail a build, and it is not an input-stated state set.\n\n| State | Meaning | Terminal |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Generated` | The document has been produced from the route registry (`ASM-BE-169`). | no |\n| `Validated` | The document parses, matches the code, and contains the Scope section. | no |\n| `Published` | The document is served by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`. | yes, for that build |\n| `Rejected` | A required check failed and the build failed. | yes, for that build |\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Generated` | `Validated` | the validation checks run | the document parses as OpenAPI 3.x (US-004-04), the documented types match the code (US-004-02), and the Scope section is present (US-005-01) |\n| `Generated` | `Rejected` | the validation checks run | the document is malformed |\n| `Generated` | `Rejected` | the validation checks run | a documented field type differs from the code (US-004-02) |\n| `Generated` | `Rejected` | the validation checks run | the Scope section is missing (US-005-01) |\n| `Validated` | `Published` | the build completes | none beyond the above. Whether a coverage below 100% also blocks this transition is unstated (`GAP-BE-177`). |\n\nA new build starts again at `Generated`. Terminal states are terminal per build, not per document.\n\n**Illegal transitions.** The lifecycle runs in the build pipeline, not behind an HTTP operation. No status or error code is defined for an illegal transition, and none is needed.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Requirements\n\nRequirement ids use the module name `CONTRACT-DOCS`.\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-001: the document is published and complete\n- **Given** notes_api is deployed,\n- **When** a client requests the OpenAPI document,\n- **Then** a valid OpenAPI 3.x JSON document is returned listing all CRUD, tagging, search, archive, and email one-time-code login (request-code and verify-code) endpoints. Only JSON is offered by this path (`ASM-BE-167`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01, BR-012, NFR-008\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_openapi_document_is_valid_and_lists_all_routes`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-002: a new endpoint is documented no later than it is callable\n- **Given** a new endpoint is added to notes_api,\n- **When** the spec is regenerated and published,\n- **Then** the new endpoint appears in the document before or at the same time it becomes callable. The mechanism is that the document and the mounted routes come from one registry (`ASM-BE-169`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01, BR-012\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_new_route_appears_in_document_on_same_build`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-003: unsupported Accept header\n- **Given** the OpenAPI document is requested with an unsupported `Accept` header,\n- **When** the request is handled,\n- **Then** the response is 406 with a clear content type for the error body. A default-format fallback is the other reading the input allows (`ASM-BE-162`, `Q-BE-161`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_unsupported_accept_returns_406`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-004: request and response schemas are documented\n- **Given** an endpoint accepts a note payload,\n- **When** its schema is inspected in the document,\n- **Then** all required fields, types and constraints are documented, taken from the owning module's operation record and not defined here. Constraints are limited to those some input states (`GAP-BE-175`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02, BR-012\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_schemas_declare_required_fields_types_constraints`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-005: optional parameters and their defaults\n- **Given** an endpoint supports optional query parameters, such as search filters,\n- **When** the schema is reviewed,\n- **Then** optional parameters are marked optional and their default values are documented.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_optional_params_marked_with_defaults`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-006: type drift fails CI\n- **Given** a documented field type is changed in code,\n- **When** the document is not updated to match,\n- **Then** the spec-validation CI check fails and flags the mismatch.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_ci_fails_on_type_mismatch`\n- **API.** none (build-time check, see `backend.contract-docs.jobs`)\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-007: validation and server errors are documented\n- **Given** an endpoint can return validation errors,\n- **When** the document is reviewed,\n- **Then** a 400 response with the error structure is documented, and every endpoint documents its 4xx and 5xx responses. The structure is the envelope in `backend.error-model` (`ASM-BE-006`, `GAP-BE-004`), referenced and not redefined here.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03, BR-012\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_every_operation_documents_400_and_5xx`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-008: authorization and login errors are documented\n- **Given** an endpoint requires authentication or authorization, or is a login endpoint,\n- **When** the document is reviewed,\n- **Then** 401 and 403 responses are documented with the expected body shape. The request-code operation documents the 429 response for more than 3 requests within 10 minutes (BR-028). The verify-code operation documents the locked-account response, 423 (BR-033). Which note and search endpoints require authentication is undecided (`Q-BE-002`, `Q-BE-003`), so no 401 or 403 is asserted for them here.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03, BR-028, BR-033\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_login_operations_document_429_and_423`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-009: the 404 documentation matches behaviour\n- **Given** an endpoint documents a 404 response,\n- **When** the documented response is checked against the actual API behaviour,\n- **Then** they match. The input sentence for this criterion is garbled (`GAP-BE-180`), so this reading is the intent that survives its text.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_documented_404_matches_runtime`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-010: complete coverage reports 100%\n- **Given** all implemented endpoints are documented,\n- **When** the coverage check runs,\n- **Then** it reports 100% coverage (NFR-008). The check counts every route in the registry, including this module's own four (`ASM-BE-163`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04, NFR-008\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_coverage_reports_100_when_complete`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-011: a missing endpoint is named\n- **Given** one endpoint is implemented but missing from the document,\n- **When** the coverage check runs,\n- **Then** it reports less than 100% and names the missing endpoint.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_coverage_names_missing_endpoint`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-012: a malformed document never yields a coverage figure\n- **Given** the spec file is malformed,\n- **When** the coverage check runs,\n- **Then** it fails with a clear parsing error and reports no coverage figure. Over HTTP the failure is a 5xx error response (`ASM-BE-165`, `Q-BE-162`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_malformed_spec_fails_with_parse_error`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-013: example payloads\n- **Given** the search and tagging endpoints are documented,\n- **When** a developer views the document,\n- **Then** an example request with its query parameters and an example response body are shown for search. Tagging operations have examples as well, and an example empty-result search response is included. All examples use synthetic values (`ASM-BE-168`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05, NFR-009\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_search_and_tagging_have_examples_including_empty_result`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-014: the data-residency note\n- **Given** the API documentation includes a data-handling note,\n- **When** an operations engineer reviews it,\n- **Then** it states that all note data is stored and served within the EU. It calls out any difference between archived and active note storage. `backend.data-lifecycle` records notes, including archived ones, as one database in one EU region, so the note states that no difference exists (`GAP-BE-178`). The same text is returned by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650` and embedded in the document (`ASM-BE-166`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06, BR-013, BR-001\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_residency_note_states_eu_and_archive_difference`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650`, \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-015: the Scope section\n- **Given** the published documentation,\n- **When** a reader views the Scope section,\n- **Then** mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded (BR-014, BR-015, BR-016). The same text is returned by `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969` and embedded in the document (`ASM-BE-166`).\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01, BR-014, BR-015, BR-016\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_scope_lists_three_exclusions`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969`, \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-016: a missing Scope section fails the build\n- **Given** the documentation build pipeline,\n- **When** the Scope section is missing from a build,\n- **Then** the documentation build fails with a lint or validation error.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_build_fails_without_scope_section`\n- **API.** none (build-time check)\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-017: changes to the exclusion list require review\n- **Given** a documentation update is proposed,\n- **When** a new out-of-scope item is added or changed,\n- **Then** the change requires review and approval before merge. The enforcement mechanism is a repository process and no input defines it (`GAP-BE-179`).\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01\n- **Verification.** (planned) none possible until the process is defined (`GAP-BE-179`)\n- **API.** none\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-018: the interface-change notice is documented\n- **Given** the login endpoints and the `pinned` flag are interface changes,\n- **When** the document is published,\n- **Then** it states that consumers must adapt (NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016). The login endpoints are additive and existing login methods remain unchanged (NFR-016; those methods are unspecified, `GAP-BE-008`).\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016, US-004-01\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_document_carries_interface_change_notice`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n\n### BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-019: examples carry no secrets or real-looking PII\n- **Given** any example in the document, the residency note or the Scope section,\n- **When** the document is published,\n- **Then** no secret, key or token appears, and email addresses are masked (NFR-018).\n- **Traces to.** NFR-018, US-004-05\n- **Verification.** (planned) `contract_docs_examples_contain_no_secrets_or_unmasked_emails`\n- **API.** \u2192 `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363`\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363` | GET | `/openapi.json` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 406, 500 | Return the published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint, including the data-residency note and Scope section. |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` | GET | `/openapi/coverage` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 500 | Report the spec-versus-route coverage check result. |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650` | GET | `/openapi/data-residency` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 500 | Return the data-residency note published with the API documentation. |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969` | GET | `/openapi/scope` | `contract-docs` | 200 | 500 | Return the Scope section listing what the API does and explicitly does not cover. |\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363` \u2014 GET `/openapi.json`\n\nReturn the published OpenAPI 3.x document covering every implemented endpoint, including the data-residency note and Scope section.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI version string of the published document (3.x).\n- `info` (object, required): API metadata (title, version) for the published document.\n- `paths` (object, required): Every implemented endpoint with its schemas, 4xx/5xx errors and examples (BR-012, NFR-008, NFR-009).\n- `components` (object, required): Shared schemas, including the error body schema.\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` \u2014 GET `/openapi/coverage`\n\nReport the spec-versus-route coverage check result.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `implemented_count` (integer, required): Number of implemented routes found in the route registry.\n- `documented_count` (integer, required): Number of those routes present in the published spec.\n- `coverage_percent` (number, required): Share of implemented endpoints present in the published spec (target 100%, NFR-008).\n- `missing_endpoints` (array, required): Implemented routes absent from the spec; the build fails when non-empty (US-004-04).\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650` \u2014 GET `/openapi/data-residency`\n\nReturn the data-residency note published with the API documentation.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `note` (string, required): Text of the data-residency note published with the API documentation (BR-013).\n\n### `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969` \u2014 GET `/openapi/scope`\n\nReturn the Scope section listing what the API does and explicitly does not cover.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `scope` (string, required): Text of the Scope section stating what the API covers.\n- `excluded` (array, required): Excluded capabilities: attachments, real-time channels, mobile-specific endpoints (BR-014 to BR-016).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-151.** After every build that reaches `Published`, every route in the registry appears in the document, or the coverage result is below 100% and names each missing route. Derived from BR-012 and US-004-04.\n- **INV-BE-152.** A document that cannot be parsed never produces a coverage percentage. Derived from US-004-04, third criterion.\n- **INV-BE-153.** No build reaches `Published` without a Scope section. Derived from US-005-01, third criterion.\n- **INV-BE-154.** The data-residency note and the Scope section served by their own operations are identical in content to the copies embedded in the document, because they share one source text (`ASM-BE-166`).\n- **INV-BE-155.** Every documented operation lists its 4xx and 5xx responses. Derived from BR-012 and US-004-03.\n- **INV-BE-156.** The four operations of this module change no state: they read and compute only. Derived from their definition as GET operations.\n- **INV-BE-157.** No example in any artefact served by this module contains a secret or an unmasked email address. Derived from NFR-018 and core rule 12.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Authorization\n\nNo role is defined by any input, and `backend.auth-model` is `BLOCKED` pending `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-002` and `Q-BE-003`. The matrix below therefore carries one assumption.\n\n| Operation | Permitted callers |\n|---|---|\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-098363` | any caller, without a session (`ASM-BE-164`) |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` | any caller, without a session (`ASM-BE-164`) |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-446650` | any caller, without a session (`ASM-BE-164`) |\n| `API-CONTRACTDOCS-206969` | any caller, without a session (`ASM-BE-164`) |\n\n**Basis.** The only input about access is US-004-01: \"client requests the OpenAPI document\". Integrators fetch it before they can hold a credential. If the session decision (`Q-BE-003`) requires a session for these operations, this section and the 401 documentation for them change (`Q-BE-163`).\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. The document, the coverage result, the residency note and the Scope section are not user-scoped resources.\n\n**Rate limiting.** No rate-limit class is stated by any input for these operations (`GAP-BE-176`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.contract-docs.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.contract-docs.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\nNo scheduled sweep, retry or background worker is required by any input. The work is build-time checks and request-time reads.\n\n| Job | Trigger | Does | Fails when | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Spec-versus-code validation | every CI build | compares documented types and responses with the code | a documented field type differs from the code (`BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-006`) | US-004-02 |\n| Coverage check | every CI build, and on demand via `API-CONTRACTDOCS-827943` | compares registry and document | the document is malformed (`BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-012`). A coverage below 100% is reported and named. Whether it also fails the build is unstated (`GAP-BE-177`). | US-004-04, BR-012 |\n| Scope lint | every documentation build | checks that the Scope section exists | the section is missing (`BE-CONTRACT-DOCS-FR-016`) | US-005-01 |\n\n**Cadence.** The inputs name \"every build\" for the scope guard (NFR-010, owned by `scope-guard`) and \"CI\" for the checks above. A run frequency beyond \"each CI build\" is not stated.\n\n**Idempotency.** All four operations are GETs and are idempotent. The coverage check is a pure computation over the current registry and document.
